 company's placement process NA
 Company / Organ isation Deloitte US India
 Profile Offered Strategy & Operations
 Group Discussion No
 Topic of GD NA
 Approximate duration of GD (in mins) NA
 Additional Comments about your GD NA
3 Personal Interview - Number of rounds
 Approximate duration of yo ur personal 1 Round: - 30 mins, 2 Round: - 1 Hour, 3
 interview (in mins) Round: - 30 mins
  Round 1: - Guesstimate and Resume based HR Questions.
 Cracking and structuring the guesstimate along with talking
 to the intervi ewer at the same time is very important.
  Guess - estimate - Market for turbans in Rajasthan (number
 and sales amount per year)
  Round 2: - Case Study: -
 A PE firm acquired another company and wants to increase
 its profits from 3% to 10%. Looking at the compan y’s
 business models and financial statements. I had to identify
 the reason for low profit margins, and decide steps to
 overcome this. Then I was asked if outsourcing is a solution
 and had to justify how outsourcing can help. After this they
 provided GDP, i nflation and growth rate data for several
 countries and based on it I had to identify the ideal
 outsourcing location. Finally, the discussion boiled down to
 which all processes can be outsourced. For this I had to
 perform some number crunching in which imp act of distance
 on revenue generated from each process was given and also
 Domain Specific Questions (if any) how important the process was for the business was
 provided.
  What are the drivers in the broadband television distribution
 market? (Case) What are the data points you need? (Case)
 An alyze data given and tell me about current industry and
 company situation. Should the company enter the market?
 What should be its strategy? (Case) Do a cost benefit analysis
 to see if the above strategy is feasible. Request for more data
 here
  Estimate the number of wheels on road in North Indore
  I provided 2 - 3 approaches (demand side: how many people
 would step out of home; supply side: road network in Indore
 etc.) After I found out the number of various vehicles on road,
 I made sure I did not forget to mu ltiply it with the respective
 number of wheels.
  If everybody in Rajasthan were allowed to carry a gun with
 them (like in the US), by how much will the number of
 murders reduce?
  I took a few seconds to think through and started giving some
 gyaan on Game Th eory, Nuclear deterrence etc. This bought
 me enough time to structure the problem. I approached the
 problem by considering the various scenarios in which there
 could be a murder and then went about estimating the
 reduction in rate due to presence of a gun with both the
 parties.
  The interviewer was definitely not looking for numbers. All he
 wanted to check was how I was approaching the problem. He
 made me think hard on both the problems and did not stop
 probing until I gave him enough number of ways of solv ing
 them.
  Q. Insurance company mostly into Auto and Home sectors was
 facing loses. The cause for the same was to be identified.
  Largely a quantitative case with big numbers. (Not complex)
 As and when I would ask for information (revenue structure,
 cost str ucture etc.), exhibits with relevant information would
 be released. I had to just make sense of the exhibits, do some
 math (combined ratio - defined in the case, expected profits)
 and move on. The process was pretty smooth since I asked
 them questions at e ach stage, gave qualitative reasons for
 the numbers and proceeded. The case ended with identifying
 one particular territory as highly loss making.
  Few questions were asked on my previous work ex and the
 compet itions that I won on this campus.
  Why I want to join Deloitte consulting HR / Current affair based questions (if  Why consulting is perfect for me. Where was my previous any) work ex and internships?
  Which industry (in Deloitte) do you feel you fit?
  Tell me something that is not in your resume?
  There were three rounds of interviews.
  HR/Guesstimate: Questions like 'run me through your
 resume, internship, case study competitions done, PORs and
 guesstimate are asked.
  Case around : They will give you a ca se. It will be a data heavy
 case and require you to propose long term and short term Addi tional Comments about your strategies to the firm in trouble. Make sure you think laterally personal interview and propose a lot of options to them. Don't deep dive into
 one option as you will be trapped.
  Partner roun d: Mostly HR based. The partner checks your "
 culture fit" in the organization. Talk to him/enjoy the
 conversation. If you make it till this round, chances are you
 will convert the company.
 Any other rounds (apart from Personal NA Interview/GD)
  Please be thoroughly prepared with your resume and try
 conversing a lot with the interviewer as it will help.
  Be very careful while dealing with guesstimates. The key is to Any Tip s/Suggestions for future practice a lot of such questions so that none takes you by candidates surprise. Try not to always start the guesstimate by the total
 population method.
  Practice demand/supply techniques of solving guesstimates.
 Keep the interviewer engaged. After arriving at the end
 figure, do propose doing a sensitivity analys is. Alter the
 assumptions and see how they affect the final answer. It will
 become your defense in case the interviewer feels your
 answer is underestimated/overestimated.
  For the Case based discussion workshop: Basic concepts of
 demand/supply, macro/micro factors shall be of help. Do
 prepare the book Case in Point and the ISB casebook
 properly. The consulting club shall forward you the same. The
 Vaults guide to case interviews is also a good read for all
 consulting interviews
 Any other comment about the  Do participate in their Case Based Workshop properly. They do
 co mpany's placement process spot people whom they definitely want to see in the
 interviews. Sort of preference order so to say. Do not however
 appear to 'desperate to participate'. In short, don' t globe.
 Allow people to speak. Do grab the opportunity to summarize
 if the panel asks for it from the group.

## Deloitte US India

**Company / Organisation:** Deloitte US India
 Profile Offered Innovation (Advisory)
 Group Discussion No
 Topic of GD NA
 Approximate duration of GD (in mins) NA
 Additional Comments about your GD NA
3 Personal Interview - Number of rounds
 Approximate duration of your personal 1 Round: - 30 mins, 2 Round: - 1 Hour, 3 Round: - 30 mins
 interview (in mins)
  Guesstimate the number of FTAs in MP, best thing about
 Indore etc. He asked me to solve the guesstimate for the
 number of Poha Jalebi plates consumed in Indore on the
 morning of December 25th 20xx from 6am to 12 noon.
  A ca se based on " Strategies adopted by a hospital to mitigate
 losses due to natural calamities". Dom ain Specific Questions (if any)
  There's a billionaire who will die in 2 years and wants to go to
 moon before he dies. You cannot say no. What will you do?
 (Again on the spot thinking. After many attempts (health
 check, send to NASA, ISRO etc. , finally nailed it by saying
 simulate the atmosphere somewhere on earth and make him
 feel like he's there)
  Since the profile was Innovation (Advisory), he asked what
 did I understand about "innovation" and what is my
 understanding of the work that the employees do at Deloitte
 Innovation Division
  She asked me to describe myself in 5 adjectives.
  She then asked me to write 5 adjectives for Deloitte. I
 deliberately linked the already mentioned 5 words about
 myself to describe the company.
 HR / Current affair based questions (if  She then asked me what are the qualities according to me,
 any) that Deloitte was looking for in candidates.
  She asked me if I could think of a better way to conduct the
 process so that the time is reduced by half and the quality is
 not compromised at the same time. I thought for a while and
 applied my OM and HR knowledge to the most I could.
  Given social, political, sports and environment what would you
 like to discuss on?
  Tech job vs consulting job, which would you choose?
  What one thing will you change in this world?
  Stress interview: It was a rapid fire round wherein each of the
 5 panelists asked at least 2 questions each back to b ack. Some
 of the questions were:
  The Partner asked: I think IPM should be closed down. Additional Comments about your Convince us that it should not. personal interview
  Partner: Dance, music, drama, swimming etc. (In the previous
 answer, I had talked about what all we learnt in IPM) are
 extremely vague fo r a student to learn at IIM. These should be
 outright removed from the curriculum. What are your views?
  Senior Manager: Your resume looks very much finance
 oriented. We are not looking for someone like you, you would
 be better at an IB. Any concerns?
  I kne w if I bogged down at this point, it would mean the end
 for me at Deloitte. I answered this saying that it only shows
 my interests and not how well I might do at any other field.
 Clearly I am not a marketing person, but my resume is just an
 example of the fact that I achieved good at what interested
 me the most at IIM Indore. I quoted from my CV and even
 otherwise that I was an all - rounder (I had also won a few
 competitions in Strategy, Quizzing, Marketing, HR and B Plan).
 The whole idea was to convince the m why I would not leave
 the job and switch for a bank later.
  Alum 01: Why should we hire you when we need just one and
 there are already two waiting outside. What is so unique
 about you?
  The answer was more or less similar to my previous one, but I
 had to market myself well again in a different manner. So I
 said some other things as well in addition to the achievements
 (like my strengths etc. ).
  HR: It is evident from your resume that you like dancing. We
 won’t ask you to dance here, but my question is: can you
 draw? (I said: yes, what would you like me to draw?). The
 five of them discussed for a moment and asked me to draw
 an airplane and I did
 Any other rounds (apart from Personal NA Interview/GD)
 Any Tips/Suggestions for future NA candidates

 company's placement process

 candidates  Give them structured answers during the cases. T ake your

 Personal Interview - Number of 3

 sigma , lean manufacturing, theory of constraints, finding
 bottleneck in a manufacturing process and various process in a
 plant.

 oriented questions in first two rounds

 improved keeping other parameters constant in OEE
  First round - mostly resume based stressing on why consulting, HR / Current affair based questions questions to check your thought process, communication skills (if any) and logical structuring.
  Partner round - Again resume based and case based. Was
 mainly about the work I did during my summers. Some routine
 questions on long term career goal, achievements, live
 projects I had done, situation based questions, competitions I
 won, extra - curricular activities.
  Why KPMG? Why Consulting? One example of I being
 ambitious, 3 characteristics that need to be there in a
 consultant.

 to other short - lists. Won't that be a disadvantage

  The interviewers asked a lot of questions about Work - ex and
 Live Projects.
  Why is mineral water bottle priced at Rs 20?
  Evaluate impact of odd even scheme in Delhi.
  Tell me the number of fans in the room without counting, the
 fox puzzle.
  They asked me calculate numbers that were mentioned in my
 resume. For eg I had mentioned savings of 80 lacs in my
 resume; so they asked me to calculate how I came up to this
 number
  Did you get a chance to go through the website of KPMG?
  How was the overall interview process?
  Do you have any questions for us?
 Additional Comments about your  A Case was given of a manufacturing company that has 5
 personal interview plants across the c ountry, all manufacturing identical products.
 Each plant has its own Accounts department, with a total of
 400 accounts staff across all plants. There is one central
 Finance department at the Headquarters that takes care of
 Cash management for the entire fi rm. The HQ feels that they
 have too many staff in accounts. So you are being consulted to
 look into reducing the numbers. Before going to the plant for a
 2 - day visit, enlist the parameters you will measure at the site
 and how?
  Case: advise an airline wheth er it should install Wi - Fi service
 with the flight
  Case: based on a case identify reasons for less market share in
 spares and also size the spares market.
  They were mainly looking for people with a consulting
 temperament and client side presentability - fit .
  Asked to use management jargons like Market Segmentation,
 Competitor Analysis etc.
  Some other situations related to different types of vehicles
 and competitor scenarios were asked.
 Any other rounds (apart from  A presentation round . We were given 30 mins to prepare 3
 Personal Interview/GD) slides ppt explaining our way ahead on a small case. Case was
 on "Improving capacity for a national transportation
 company". We were asked to make any assumption as per our
 requirement.
  Problem statement: "A large trans port company has a national
 network. Operating at 70% capacity, desires to achieve 90%+.
 How this company should approach this challenge?
  The company was more interested in the approach for the Any Tips/Suggestions for future case study round. candidates
  Be well prepared with the points mentioned in your resume.
 Have 2 - 3 well prepared stories which will differentiate you
 from others and prove your strengths.
  Try to get familiar with Case GD's. Read case - in - point it will
 help you to structure cases.
  Be men tally prepared as process is very rigorous. Be confident
 whatever you say.
  Think out loud
  Listen to the questions properly. Do not jump the gun.
  Do not globe around especially for questions like Why KPMG
  Be interactive and ask questions If you have a doubt

 company's placement process

 business flow. apart from this be confident, don't think you

**Company / Organisation:** Motilal Oswal Finances
 Profile Offered Finance
 Group Discussion
 Topic of GD  BR ICS ( Brazil. Russia, India, China, South Africa) are strong and
 PIGS ( Portugal, Italy, Greece, Spain) are flying.
  Is India Intolerant?
 Approximate duration of GD (in mins) NA
 Additional Comments about your GD  Before starting the GD, it is important to understand the topic
 very clearly as one of the participant thought BRICS as "Bricks"
 and he started speaking on something else. Panellists were
 looking for good knowledge in economics as it was necessary
 for the role as well as communication skills
 Pers onal Interview - Number of rounds 2 Interviews
 Approximate duration of your personal First interview - 30 mins, Second interview - 25 mins
 interview (in mins)
 Domain Specific Questions (if any)  Covered questions ranging from valuation to international
 f inance. They asked about the devaluation of China's currency
 and what is its impact on India and how India should counter
 this. They asked many questions regarding valuation of a
 company: different methods, ROE, ROIC etc.
  HR interview, asked about my famil y and my interests. Asked
 why I want to join the company, my long term goals.
 HR / Current affair based questions (if  It was a HR interview and they were looking for culture fitness.
 any) Read about the company very well.
  It was conducted by the HR Head over skype. Started with 'tell
 me about yourself' then asked about the Indian Economy,
 falling oil prices and CAD. Asked if I knew about the company's
 revenue, PAT and share price.
  Indian Economy, falling oil prices and CAD. Asked if I knew
 about the comp any's revenue, PAT and share price.
 Additional Comments about your  They were looking for commitment and desire to learn in the
 personal interview firm, they were not looking for any expertise in financial
 markets or products but were grilling upon anythi ng written
 about finance in CV.
  They asked me if I know anything about trading and I honestly
 said no. I said that I don't have any idea about trading and nor
 does any of my family member.
  They were cool with it, so don't lie if you don't know anything.
 Just accept it otherwise they will grill you like anything. Also,
 my internship was in operations and I went in too much detail
 about it which made them slightly irritated.
  During the final HR interview, I told the company's revenue
 wrong. However, it went unnoticed probably because I told it
 confidently.
 Any other rounds (apart from Personal  There was an aptitude test to shortlist students for GD
 Interview/GD)  Aptitude test consisting of quant and verbal like CAT
 Any Tips/Suggestions for future  Know well about Financial Valuation concepts, good knowledge
 candidat es of current economic conditions of various countries and
 analytics domain background
 Any other comment about the  Be calm, very calm. This interview was my fourth interview for
 company's placement process that day. I was so exhausted that I just sat in front of the
 interviewer and was talking informally without any pressure. It
 was very casual.
  Be thorough with each and every point on your resume. Read
 about the authors if you have mentioned reading as your
 hobby.

 topic.

 beforehand to avoid any fish market in the GD. Our GD was
 very peaceful, everyone got to say his/her points. This was not
 always the case, some GD's did become a little messy, with
 candidates interjecting each other too often. From each GD
 mostly one and maximum two candidates made it to the
 second round.

**Company / Organisation:** OLA Cabs
 Profile Offered Program Manager

 Topic of GD NA
 Approximate duration of GD (in mins) NA
 Additional Comments about your GD NA
 Personal Interview - Number of rounds 2 (1 technical+1 HR kind)
 Approximate duration of your personal 35mins + 10mins interview (in mins)
 Guesstimates –

 day,
 Domain Specific Questions (if any)  No. of taxis in Indore, etc.

 Seden) in Different cities (Metro or Tier I & II)
  Why OLA?
 HR / Current affa ir based questions (if  Why OLA was in news recently?
 any)  What's your biggest achievement in IIM Indore,
  Location constraints
  I think more than the final solution, it's the approach that you
 ad opt while solving a problem. Even if you are not able to come
 to a specific figure, make sure whatever you have done you can Additional Comments about your explain to the interviewer and not just get disappointed in the personal interview middle of the interview. One single act of yours during an hour -
 lon g interview can be enough to get you selected. So, try to stay
 calm and focused as much as possible.
 Any other rounds (apart from Personal NA Interview/GD)
  Prepare for operations based problems
  One must be abl e to communicate effectively as well as
 holistically. Try to have healthy discussion among your peers
 on all kinds of topics because peer learning is one of the most
 significant part of your MBA life.
 Any Tips/Suggestions for future  Always respect others' opinion even if it's different from
 candidates yours as there is always two sides to the coin.
  Build up towards your personality and make your presence
 felt. Work towards developing confidence as this is something
 you can't fake and no matter how much you try, if you don't
 have that belief in you rself it will definitely show up in the
 interviews.
 Any other comment about the  Most Companies expect an all - round kind of a guy who is not
 company's placement process only good technically but who also has a lighter side to him.
 One should be polite and pleasant while dealing with the
 company representatives, but at the same time you shouldn't
 hesitate to take an opposite stand from what the interviewer
 expects as this will exhibit the leader in you. There are times
 when companies deliberately put you under pressure to find
 out how much stress you can take. Now, as far as location
 issues are concerned I would suggest that it's always better to
 be open to any location in the country at least for few initial
 years. It's not worth to jeopardize a good opp ortunity just
 because you are not getting a location of your choice.

 personal interview written in your CV.

  Prepare HR questions well as recruiters wil l tell you again &

  Asked abo ut my college (graduation)?
  Why did you switch you job (as I switched my first job after 17
 months)? Discussion on this went on for quite some time and the
 interviewer was trying to judge whether I will stay in the
 company (if selected) or will switch very soon. Try to justify this
 question with proper reasons and don't give reasons like I
 switched my job because I was getting a higher salary or a
 location of my choice. I justified this question by saying that I
 wanted to explore a new industry and wanted t o learn new
 products/ technology.
  Asked questions on internship like what were your
 findings/suggestions to top management etc.?
  Asked about live projects - I did three live projects out of which
 he asked about two?
  Asked about cloud technology and recent trends in cloud
 computing (this question came as I had written a thesis on Major
 inhibitors to cloud computing)?
  In the end he asked if I had any questions for him. I had a doubt
 regarding the JD so I went on to ask that.

 pe rsonal interview they want people with interest in technology.

 candidates  Prepare your CV well.

 (mentioned corporate banking, got asked about corporate

 Additional Comments a bout your  Interviews were mainly resume - based and IT domain - specific.
 personal interview  In two rounds details regarding the internship was asked
  The interviewers were very pleasant, but asked for explanation of
 many phrases in the resume. The interviews were more focused
 on technology trends and willingness to learn.
  When detailed about the organisational structure of EBA with
 different functions being technology development, business
 development and hand holding the clients in usage (don't
 remember the exact function names but broadly the meanings
 are the same as I have mentioned), I pre - empted a possible
 question and asked will it be possible to have a role operating
 across all 3 domains. This created a positive vibe with the
 interviewer and he gave a v ery detailed and informal explanation
 of the operations and future scope of the unit.
 Any other rounds (apart from Personal  There is an online automated video interview round, before the
 Interview/GD) on - campus interviews. 3 questions of 5 minutes each w ere asked
 in this round:1. Tell us something about yourself.2. Describe a
 situation where you exhibited leadership skills and motivated
 people to achieve the goals.3. How do you react when you do not
 achieve the desired level of success despite putting in your 100%
 effort?
 Any Tips/Suggestions for future  Try and engage the interviewer in a conversation.
 candidates  Even when you can't give an exact answer, coming up with a
 variety of viewpoints on a problem, shows your depth of thinking.
  Don't get tense an d believe in your strengths and try to direct the
 interview to your strong points.
  The interviewer was more than gracious in putting me at ease
 when I admitted to not knowing something.
  The process began in the early morning and I had to wait for 5
 hours b efore my first interview call came but it doesn't affect your
 chances as all that matters is your performance during the time
 period of the interview. So don't let your morale go down.
 Any other comment about the  The intervie wers were pretty friendly and were eager to have a
 company's placement process frank conversation. It was a pleasant experience.

## MAQ Software

**Company / Organisation:** MAQ Software
 Profile Offered Associate Project Manager
 Group Discussion Yes
 Topic of GD Case Based
 1. Project Planning
 2. Project managemen t - How will you accommodate change requests
 in already scheduled delivery plan
 3. Agile software methodology followed in a firm.
  Two features have been scheduled for the month. Now client is
 asking for two more features. You are expected to discuss with
 the group and see if it is possible to accommodate.
 Solution expected for agile:
  They expected numbers and not just vague discussion. The
 case can be solved in three steps which none of us thought
 about
  Step 1: Effort in person - days required to implement the
 c urrent two features
  Step 2: Effort in person - days required to implement the
 two additional features
 Approximate duration of GD (in mins) 25  - 30 Step 3: Percentage change the client is asking for based on above

## Mydala

**Company / Organisation:** Mydala
 Profile Offered Product Manager
 Group Discussion No
 Topic of GD NA
 Approximate duration of GD (in mins) NA
 Additional Comments about your GD NA
 Personal Interview - Number of rounds 3
 Appr oximate duration of your personal 2 hours
 interview (in mins)
 Domain Specific Questions (if any)  Compared to competitors in India, do you know if mydala has
 anything unique (coupon industry).
 HR / Current affair based questions (if  How will you fit as a product manager to our company?
 any)  Do you have any relevant work experience?
  If I give my IT team to you, what will you do as a product manager.
  How will you go about doing your job, what do you think are the
 various aspects of it?
 Additional C omments about your  The above 3 general questions were asked in the first round. It
 personal interview was very casual and lasted only about 15 minutes’ max (approx.).
 The only reason I got selected was because I aggressively sold
 myself, telling them that despite absence of work experience
  I have a relevant internship. I somehow managed to bring in
 actual suggestions for their website while answering the first
 question. Looks like these simple things and the way I
 communicated actually made a difference, since other people
 with relevant experience didn't get through the first round -
 something that came across as a surprise.
  Also, I had the 2nd telephonic round in my personal cell phone.
 My number was given to them just 10 or 20 minutes before the
 intervi ew and that was when I was notified of the same. It just
 didn't work and there was a lot of call cuts as well as hardly any
 clarity due to phone - signal problems. So it's advisable to have
 these rounds in the placement office itself.
 Any other rounds (apa rt from Personal NA
 Interview/GD)
 Any Tips/Suggestions for future  Google and read well about all the things given in the JD. In this
 candidates case, it was things like online marketing, SEO techniques, etc.
  Know the jargons, technical terms and do try to use them
 wherever it fits.
  And in general, for a profile like product manager, just google the
 term and read up some links, blogs etc. about what is expected of
 the profile. Helped me a lot!
 Any other comment about the company's  I n the last round which I originally though was just a formality, the
 placement process director ended up asking me about what I did in the internship
 and also asked if I had any suggestions for their website.
  Since I luckily ended up getting at least a day of gap between e ach
 round, I had come up with more suggestions for their website in
 the meantime. And that came in handy. I came to know several
 days later that he ended up noting down every single suggestion
 that I made and even sent it to the product development team.
 S o these things are taken rather seriously here and therefore you
 can't bluff. That's about it

**Company / Organisation:** Google
 Profile Offered Account Strategist
 G roup Discussion No
 Topic of GD NA
 Approximate duration of GD (in mins) NA
 Additional Comments about your GD NA
4 Personal Interview - Number of rounds
 Approximate duration of your personal 140 mins (35 mins each) interview (in mins)
  Questions related to AdWords were asked.
  Case based: You are managing a set of AdWords accounts. You
 have been given a target revenue of $100000 for the quarter.
 Half the quarter is over and as of now; you are set to achiev e
 only 50% of the target. What are the steps you will take to
 identify the root causes of bad performance and what are the
 steps you will take to fix the issues?
  If you are given $ 1 billion to invest on behalf of Google, where
 will you invest?
  Case base d: They gave a current problem that Google is facing.
 It is related to assigning priorities and solving technical/non -
 technical issues raised by Ad reviewers while keeping the
 bandwidth of the developers in mind.
  Case based: This was related to demand for ecasting. Let's say
 you are in charge of a call centre. On particular days, the
 demand will be high. However, you have to make sure that
 you don't miss your SLAs. How will you tweak your staffing
 pattern to meet demand while keeping costs down? Domai n Specific Questions (if any)  What are t he KPIs for a call - centre - like organisation?
  Guesstimate: Let's say 15% of all business listings on Google
 Maps are non - compliant. Such listings affect the users in some
 way or the other. How many users will be affected because of
 this?
  Suggest some ways to make Google Maps more accurate in
 terms of listings. That is, how to validate the information on
 the listing?
  If you have to choose on Google product and make it more
 engaging to users, which product will it be? Why?
  Suggest ways to increase user eng agement on Google Plus.
  Have you worked on predictive data modelling ? Explain the
 problem and how you solved it.
  Explain the different joins that are possible on databases,
 primarily inner join and outer join.
  Many resume based questions.
  Some basic st atistics related questions
  Have you ever been in a situation where you could not meet a
 target? When? How did you tackle the situation? What steps
 will you take to avoid such a scenario now?
  Have you been in a situation where a member of your team
 was not performing well? How did you handle that scenario?
  If you are new in a team, what steps will you take to mingle
 HR / Current affair based questions (if with others?
 any)  You are a new manager on a team and your style of
 management is radically different from the previous
 manager's. The team members are having a tough time
 adjusting to your methods. What will you do?
  Tell me a characteristic, which, if present on this job, you will
 refuse to take the job.
  Why do you want to join Google?
  They gave ample time to think and answer.
 Additional Comme nts about your  Even though each interview lasted about 30 to 40 mins, there
 personal interview was no stress whatsoever. They made sure the interview was
 more like a conversation rather than an interrogation.
 A ny other rounds (apart from Personal NA Interview/GD)
  Have a good grasp on AdWords concepts (It is easy to get
 certified by copying answers from the internet but the
 certification will not help unless you know the concepts).
  Try to offer insights in addition to plain answers. Even as they
 tell something, feel free to offer your views
  (if you are confident).
  Feel free to ask questions to understand their question
 properly. This will engage the interviewer and also send a
 positive signal about your interest.
  Be very thorough on what's on your resume. They run a timer
 to make sure the interview doesn't last beyond 30 to 40 mins.
 Any Tips/Suggestions for future So you would not want to waste time by dragging on
 candidates questions pertaining to the resume .
  Read up about Google and its products. They ask quite a few
 questions on those.
  At the end of each round, they open up for questions. Never
 say you don't have any questions!! Make sure to prepare 8
 decent questions in advance so that you can ask 2 each a t the
 end of each round.
  Begin and finish the interview with firm handshakes. It may
 just be a hygiene factor but you never know!
  Watch the movie 'The Internship' if you have time. It's about
 work at Google. It's not mandatory to watch but it might help
 pi ck up a few points for some HR questions.
 Any other comment about the NA company's placement process

## Nykaa

**Company / Organisation:** Nykaa
 Profile Offered Sales and Marketing
 Group Discussion Yes
 Topic of GD  Case based GD; A HBR case on channel - management ( online
 vs offline) was given. Panellist s were testing the analytical
 skills. They were grilling on the financial analysis part.
 Approximate duration of GD (in mins) 20
 Additional Comments about your GD  Called in groups of 10 - 11 and asked to introduce ourselves in
 the following Way - Name, Co mpany and the role that I would
 like to work for after 10 years and why. Be honest with the
 answer, it need not be the same company or the industry.
  After this we were given an HBR case on Vita company which
 we were supposed to read and analyse in 30 - 35 mi n in a
 separate room and prepare answers of the three questions
 given by them. The case was based on marketing investment
 decision - whether to invest in stores or online sale. We had to
 calculate the projected revenue for the company for next 3
 years and the break - even period in both the cases. We were
 supposed to present the case analysis in groups in front of
 them and the way of presentation was left upon us to decide.
 My group chose to give chance to each of the 6 members in
 our team. The panellists we re mainly looking for logical
 thinking ability, students who were good with numbers and
 were able to explain things based on exhibits, case facts and
 valid assumptions. They were also looking for good
 communication skills and confidence. They grilled us on the
 break - even calculation for both the cases. For one the case we
 failed to answer as 30 min was too less to read and analyse a
 15 - page case. They gave us an extra 5 minutes’ time to move
 out, discuss in group and then come up with a feasible
 solution af ter which we did come up with a logical answer.
  Where do you see yourself after 10 years? Don't you think you
 are more of an ops guy who can deal with supply chain and
 logistics? So, why marketing? A few questions related to
 family background as well
 Per sonal Interview - Number of rounds 1
 Approximate duration of your personal 25 mins.
 interview (in mins)
 Domain Specific Questions (if any) NA
  Tell me about yourself.
  Have you heard of Nykaa before, why d o you want to join this
 company and changes that you would suggest in its app?
  What do you want to do in your life?
 HR / Current affair based questions (if  Your profile is oriented towards product management as you any)
 have worked in R&D, why have you applied for marketing
 role?
  Asked, if I would be willing to work for product management
 role instead of marketing.

 GD round.
  Questions on family background.
  Some question based on location - do you have any relatives
 in Mumbai and would you b e willing to work here?
  Where do you see yourself after 10 years?
  Don't you think you are more of an ops guy who can deal with
 supply chain and logistics? So, why marketing?
  A few questions related to family background as well.
  Tell about your experience of the case.
  Why did you choose Product Manager? You are a product
 person not a marketing person

  They were looking for creative and innovating thinking ability,

## Snapdeal

**Company / Organisation:** Snapdeal
 Profile Offered Supply Chain
 G roup Discussion Yes
 Topic of GD Logical puzzle
 Approximate duration of GD (in mins) 20
 Additional Comments about your GD  Advanced level logical question to be solved as a group.
  The case was somewhat similar to the logical reasoning based
 questions we get in the CAT, only it was way too much tougher
 (because of more no. of possible combinations). And then
 they've given very few info cheats making it impossible to
 solve.
  Each candidate got 2/3 info cheats (roughly 10 participants)
  The trick was how as a group we work towards finding
 solution. It was an unsolvable problem but intent and
 approach was what they were looking for (I guess)
  They did not have any particular number to pick up from the
 group. Except one person everybody from the grou p got
Personal Interview - Number of rounds 3 selected
 Approximate duration of your personal 45 min each interview (in mins)
 Domain Specific Questions (if any)  Questions of Reverse logistics
  One case - study to start with. Then there was
 probabi lity/distributions related question - "Will the student
 coming after me will have CGPA more than me or less?" Then
 some follow - up questions (conceptual only, no real
 calculations were necessary)
  Then "how we can find what are the factors to be considered
 wh ile selecting candidates" (regression). This went on more
 like a discussion
  Explaining one of the projects I worked on during work ex.
 (was somewhat related to analytics)
  What are number of shuttlecocks sold in India in a day? (I
 mentioned badminton as a h obby) - was looking for correct
 assumptions, interrupted numerous times, saying approach is
 completely wrong or some assumptions are wrong or some
 assumptions are missing  Latest trends and happenings in e - comm erce industry
  Why did I want to join analytics? Then explained the profiles
 available and ask whether I was comfortable with it. Some
 more follow - up questions on similar line
 HR / Current affair based questions (if  "Stress Interview" finding faults in my resume, why I might not any) be good fit for the company and stressed on that e.g. never
 stayed in Delhi or that far from home, so he was persistent
 that I will not be able to handle fast paced life of Delhi. And
 linked some of the mistakes I did in assumptions with life and
 pressurized me on those
 Additional Comments about your  Guesstimates, Solving a real case scenario, details about work
 personal interview ex
  Interview 1 was purely technical, looking for subject related
 knowledge and skills
  Interview 2 was completely about company fit (probably
 toughest stage because many people got rejections here)
  Guesstimates were a way to make the student focus on
 Any other rounds (apart from Personal something else and then check his HR attributes by stressing
First round was an ap titude test Interview/GD) him/her

 throughout. Also, please be prepared for a last stage stress
 interview
  You need to know the profiles you are applying for well and
 Any Tips/Suggestions for future pr epare hard for the topic.
 candidates  Though questions were very practical and concept based, deep
